# Microstructure monitor: 2026-09-01

Market metrics are equal-weight security averages.

- liquidity_regime: normal
- trade_size_regime: concentrated
- orderflow_regime: buy
- impact_regime: normal
- institution_proxy_regime: strong
- intraday_regime: balanced
- overall: TRANSITION
